- **tax-audit**: Review an eTaxNBR (Bangladesh NBR) e-Return and tax computation for one TIN — log in, read the live draft, verify the slab and rebate math against the portal's own numbers, and report ranked, quantified ways to reduce the net tax payable. - **tax-policy**: Use this playbook when the event is federal or state tax legislation, Treasury/IRS regulation, or court tax ruling affecting healthcare issuer cashflow.
